## 26 November 2026 · Which Kv1.5 measurements remain available for FR-63

Longobardo’s drug-free tail-current decay is a candidate for a registered comparison **inside the existing pH window**. At pH 7.4 and 6.5 the experimenters fitted the fast and slow decay times and their amplitudes. They estimated the activation midpoints from the tail amplitudes immediately after the capacitive transient. Only those midpoints set the Cells pH coefficient. The decay components share the recording protocol and may share cells with the calibration. The Lean register and drivers contain no score for them. D399 keeps FR-63 as a calibration report while the lead considers this readout. [Longobardo et al., Fig. 1, Methods and Results](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC1572951/#fig1) · [D399](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/378559e8ecfe6c485256af6cbb0d2eac843b335b/docs/DECISIONS.md#L13512-L13517)

| Preparation and midpoint calibration | Additional published measurement | Use by Cells |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Trapani and Korn, HEK293, 10 mM external K, pH 7.3 and 6.8 | WT Fig. 6A also labels pH 6.2, 8.0 and 8.5. The caption leaves external K unspecified. The Methods describe a zero-added-K control and the Results discuss using 10 mM K to isolate the shift. Fig. 6C normalizes the same midpoint series to a fit across pH. | All three extra levels are outside the window. Fig. 1B’s 75 ms step at 0 mV supplied no fit input. The 21 November replay already scored the acid/control current, 0.745 ± 0.019 (SEM, eight cells), against 0.72131 from Fig. 1F. [Figs. 1 and 6](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC1302603/#fig6) · [Recorded score](#kv15-ph-activation) |
| Longobardo et al., Ltk− cells, 4 mM external K, pH 7.4 and 6.5 | Fig. 1B reports an untreated midpoint at pH 10.0 (13 cells, CAPS buffer). Fig. 2 also shows a measured group near pH 5.5 (five cells) and uses pH 10 as its reference. Its 6.5 and 7.4 markers each have 21 observations, whereas the pooled Fig. 1 results give 27 and 38. The extra acidic group’s bath details and overlap are unspecified. | Both additional pH levels would test extrapolation. The +60 mV activation time constants at the two calibration pH values are already public in the Lean register as descriptive evidence. The paired replay reads the midpoints. [Fig. 1 and Results](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC1572951/#fig1) · [Fig. 2 artwork](https://cdn.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/pmc/blobs/78e5/1572951/bace95444e74/134-0704251f2.jpg) · [Replay inputs](checks/paired_law_replays.py#L124-L139) |
| Kehl et al., HEK293, 3.5 mM external K, pH 7.4 and 6.4 | Figs. 3–4 measure relative maximal conductance. At 3.5 mM K, Fig. 4 covers only pH 6.4 relative to 7.4. The Fig. 10 Results mention an approximate ionic activation shift at pH 5.9 without a curve, explicit bath K, sample count or error. Fig. 9 has a 3.5 mM K saturated-tail recording at pH 5.9. | The Fig. 2 midpoints set the shift. The earlier replay already compared the 3.5 mM conductance endpoint from Fig. 2. The in-window conductance observations at intermediate pH in Figs. 3–4 use other K baths. [Kehl et al., Fig. 2](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC2290311/#fig02) · [Figs. 9–10](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC2290311/#fig09) · [Recorded comparison](#paired-law-replays) |

<a id="fr63-kehl-conductance"></a>

**Kehl’s pH levels for FR-63 row 6.** Figure 3 uses 0, 5 and 140 mM external K. Across those series the marker positions on the log proton-concentration axis correspond approximately to pH 5.5, 5.9, 6.4, 6.65, 6.8, 6.9, 7.4 and 8.4. The series use different subsets. The pH ≈6.8 marker belongs to the nominally K-free bath with N-methyl-D-glucamine. The Methods specify pH 7.4 as the normalization reference. [Fig. 3 artwork](https://cdn.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/pmc/blobs/189e/2290311/56287b6787a9/tjp0541-0009-f3.jpg) · [Methods and Fig. 3](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC2290311/#fig03)

Figure 4 labels pH 5.9, 6.4 and 6.9. At 5.9 and 6.9 the K series uses 0, 5, 20 and 140 mM. At 6.4 it uses 0, 1, 3.5, 5, 10, 20, 80 and 140 mM. One caption sentence prints 5.4 for the lowest series. The figure labels and Results both give 5.9. Row 6 remains an indication for how available conductance varies with pH and K. Neither figure supplies a third activation midpoint in the calibration bath. [Fig. 4 artwork](https://cdn.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/pmc/blobs/189e/2290311/079427cadbee/tjp0541-0009-f4.jpg) · [Fig. 4 and Results](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC2290311/#fig04) · [FR-63 candidate 6](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/03d96f54/docs/FR63_CALIBRATION_REPORT.md#4-held-out-quantities)

For the tail-decay candidate, the two protocols use a 250 ms depolarization and repolarize to **−40 mV at pH 7.4 and −30 mV at pH 6.5**. The fast and slow time constants have 38 and 24 recordings at those pH levels. The paper gives a prepulse family and describes the two-exponential fit. A registration would specify which trace to compare, the fitting interval, component ordering and criterion for a resolvable second component before scoring the simulated current. It would retain the native activation midpoint (−6.0 mV versus −14.2 mV in the control preparation). The published outcomes are visible to evidence reviewers. A new test would assess the channel with its declared shift and kinetics. [Study protocol](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC1572951/#fig1) · [Native gate and voltage domain](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/ff15f0e766f8ad3e9a4c81df2c298459c9133d08/src/cells/laws/channels/kv1p5_mh.py#L20-L42)

<a id="fr55-sk2-closing"></a>

## 26 November 2026 · SK2 closing at two calcium levels

The search for FR-55 found two within-laboratory comparisons in recombinant rat SK2. One followed individual openings at three maintained calcium levels. The other recorded macroscopic current decay after two different calcium pulses ended in a common wash. Neither gives two macroscopic closing rates with different known calcium levels **during** closing. D390 keeps FR-55 closed pending that evidence. [D390 decision](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/9b6191bdf0800d70dcae81107378401ab3c8b474/docs/DECISIONS.md#L13251) · [Existing Cells rise-time comparison](#paired-transport-and-membrane-measurements)

In one excised rat SK2 patch at −80 mV, Hirschberg et al. held calcium at 0.4, 0.6 and 1.0 µM during entire recordings. At 0.4 and 1.0 µM the short open lifetime was 1.1 ms at both levels. The long lifetime was 13.5 and 11.9 ms, respectively. Long openings became more common, from 49% to 66%. Figure 4 gives no per-fit uncertainty. Across the five patches in Fig. 5 the authors found little change in either component lifetime over 0.4–30 µM. The separate macropatch experiment in Fig. 13A gave **57.3 ± 11.1 ms** after a 9.5 → 0 µM step (SD, six jumps in two patches). The 76 ms decay in Fig. 13B is a model prediction. [Hirschberg et al., Figs. 4, 5 and 13](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC2217120/)

Pedarzani et al. recorded untreated SK2 currents after 0.5 and 10 µM calcium pulses. Both pulses ended in the same EGTA-buffered wash without added calcium. At 0.5 µM the illustrative trace took **about 40 ms** to fall to 1/e of its excursion. At 10 µM the corresponding trace gave **about 32 ms**, and the authors reported a mean of **39.4 ± 4.1 ms** (SD, seven experiments). The graph reads have about ±5 ms reading precision. The paper gives no absolute low-pulse group mean and leaves patch identity across the two panels unspecified. These examples show recovery on a similar timescale. They cannot establish a difference between pulse levels or a closing rate at either pulse concentration. Figure 2B reports the effect of EBIO. The approximately 80 ms low-dose result in Oliver et al. is a rise time. Pedarzani shares investigators with each earlier study. [Pedarzani et al., Fig. 2](https://doi.org/10.1074/jbc.M010001200) · [Published figure](https://web.archive.org/web/20180724072427id_/http://www.jbc.org/content/276/13/9762.full.pdf#page=3) · [Readings and scale](checks/data/fr55_sk2_closing.json) · [Oliver et al., Fig. 3](https://doi.org/10.1016/S0896-6273(00)81197-6)

For the Cells two-state law, a decay to a nonzero calcium level measures the relaxation time τ = 1/(a + b), where a is the opening rate and b the closing rate. An absolute steady open fraction at that level would allow b = (1 − P∞)/τ. The two pulse washouts constrain recovery in their common final bath. The stationary single-channel recordings constrain the component lifetimes and their weights. The earlier D351 indication about transferring one closing rate across preparations stands. [Cells proof and biological comparison](claims/regulated-state-response.md)

<a id="fr51-concentration-bounds"></a>

## 24 November 2026 · How concentration changes the flux of a blocked channel

FR-51 uses the K or other permeant ion’s reversal potential as one possible reference for its blocker. At fixed membrane voltage, changing that ion’s concentration changes both the free outward flux and the fraction that stays unblocked. With $b=RT/(zF)$ in volts, $V_{ref}=V-b(\ln c_o-\ln c_i)$ has derivatives $-b/c_o$ outside and $+b/c_i$ inside. Lean proves that each absolute flux derivative is bounded by the unblocked fraction times the direct constant-field coefficient, plus $|J_{free}|\,S\,|b|/c$ at the endpoint, where $S$ bounds the blocker's voltage slope. It reuses the earlier blocker and the actual positive Bernoulli evaluator. Voltage, gate state, blocker dose, temperature and the opposite endpoint are fixed. Each of the four public theorems reports only `propext`, `Classical.choice` and `Quot.sound`. [Lean proof](CellsProofs/Transport/BlockMaterialSlope.lean) · [Native terms](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/796f04fad35ce2bc80f256024dc01b44cc9b705b/src/cells/models/surface_pump_v3.py#L732-L792)

The CPU float64 replay ran `resolved_pump_tissue@3` and the `resolved_pump_composite@1` wrapper on frozen main `796f04fa`. The independent reference at `1360c925` supplies closed-form slopes checked by a second numerical route. All eight registered criterion E cases and the extra doubled-unit case passed at both ends. The nine cases span 3,273 distinct in-range case–voltage combinations, or 6,546 comparisons. The smallest bound divided by the reference magnitude was 0.9999999999999926, within the registered relative tolerance of 10⁻¹². Nine absolute-voltage cases gave zero for the additional material terms throughout 3,270 combinations. Their ordinary concentration dependence remains in the existing material rule. A separate chloride reversal control checked the sign (−47.86 mV) and both flux derivatives by automatic differentiation at 37 voltages. [Replay](checks/block_concentration.py) · [Tagged reference and method](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/1360c925e13b72089a567b639caa22c2e26d9c53/validation/fr51/references/README.md#L74-L101)

Both registered fault controls failed at both ends as intended. In B1 at +50 mV the outside derivative magnitude was 0.01066163 and the full bound was 0.01077281 fmol/s per mM. Omitting the change through reversal left 0.00005559, about 192 times too small. Reusing the 5.4 mM reversal in the 10.8 mM condition gave a bound as small as 47.2% of the outside derivative. These are synthetic fault controls. The registered biological comparison for the Panama potassium series is separate. [Registered criterion E](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/796f04fad35ce2bc80f256024dc01b44cc9b705b/validation/fr51/REGISTRATION.md#L182-L195) · [Fault controls](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/796f04fad35ce2bc80f256024dc01b44cc9b705b/validation/fr51/plants.py#L174-L197)

To reproduce the 24 November record, run `JAX_PLATFORMS=cpu JAX_ENABLE_X64=1 python -B checks/block_concentration.py "$REF_RESULTS"` from `lean/` at driver revision `dc07534d`, with engine `796f04fa` and its `src/` on `PYTHONPATH`. Set `REF_RESULTS` to the `validation/fr51/references/results` directory from the separate `fr51-references-2026-11-24` tag. [FR-64 replay](#fr64-block-replays)

<a id="fr65-swelling-turnover"></a>

## 24 November 2026 · What swelling experiments measure about turnover

For amount $N$ and positive water volume $V$, the endpoint change in concentration splits into net solute change per final volume and dilution of the starting pool. Its instantaneous slope is $(P-L)/V-(N/V)\dot V/V$ for gross production $P$ and loss $L$. The two Lean proofs establish these measurement identities. On `796f04fa` the definition test verified that turnover refused composition with osmotic water. The FR-65 landing on 26 November admits this combination and tests separate amount and volume readouts across restart. The preparation-matched biological replay remains open. [Readout proofs](CellsProofs/Chemistry/TurnoverVolume.lean) · [Earlier refusal test](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/796f04fad35ce2bc80f256024dc01b44cc9b705b/tests/unit/test_secreted_species.py#L171-L185) · [FR-65 landing](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/8d3f211a3d6a1588ff07d784fde65db9bfe8255c/docs/DECISIONS.md#L13466-L13477)

| Preparation and readout | Observed result | What it establishes |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Isolated rat hepatocytes, ATP amount and cell water | ATP amount rose slightly with swelling. Concentration calculated using the 45-minute water measurement fell by up to about 10% without added amino acids. | ATP dilution can coexist with a gain in ATP amount. The concentration is derived from the other measurements. [Meijer et al., Figs. 1 and 3E–F](https://doi.org/10.1111/j.1432-1033.1993.tb18053.x) |
| Trout red cells, taurine and cell water per g dry solids | During recovery from the initial swelling in the sodium bath, taurine fell by 42.97 ± 1.87 µmol/g and water by 0.37 ± 0.02 g/g (SEM, 21 experiments). At 60 min cell volume was still 116% of its pre-shock value. | Dry-mass normalization establishes a net loss of intracellular taurine. The water loss refers to recovery from the swollen state. Gross transport and degradation remain unidentified. [Guizouarn et al., Fig. 1 and Table 1](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC2269780/) |
| Rat liver proteolysis under lower osmolarity | With no added amino acids, isolated hepatocytes accumulated 15.5 ± 1.2 and 15.5 ± 0.9 µmol valine/g dry cells at 90 min at 70 and 120 mM NaCl (SEM, 4 and 8 preparations). Adding the physiological amino-acid mixture inhibited breakdown by about 40% and 15% relative to the respective controls. Perfused livers from fed rats showed reduced release of labelled leucine with swelling. | The isolated cells came from fasted rats. The perfusion study measured water and label release in separate groups under matched protocols. A common quantitative clearance rate or a universal response to swelling remains open. [Meijer et al., Fig. 2B and Table 2](https://doi.org/10.1111/j.1432-1033.1993.tb18053.x) · [Häussinger et al., Fig. 1](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/1645299/) |

The trout record is the same dataset used for osmotic water. The ATP and proteolysis panels in the hepatocyte study are one publication, and ATP amount, protein breakdown and taurine loss are separate observables. The paired register keeps the conflicting proteolysis directions and the unresolved gross turnover comparison visible. [Liver data and assay definitions](checks/data/liver_swelling_turnover.json) · [Trout data](checks/data/guizouarn2000_water_solutes.json)

<a id="fr51-weighted-block"></a>

## 24 November 2026 · FR-51 equilibrium channel block

Lean proves the unblocked fraction lies in [0, 1] for any finite collection of modes with nonnegative weights of positive total, positive dissociation constants and a nonnegative blocker concentration. With zero blocker the fraction is one and its voltage slope is zero. Cells gives all modes the same blocker side and charge sign. Under that condition its fraction-slope bound equals the absolute derivative at every voltage. For blocked current the product rule gives the stage bound conditional on a valid conductance bound for the free current. These derivatives hold concentrations, temperature and availability fixed. The seven public theorems use the pinned Mathlib and the local single-site occupancy definition. Each reports only `propext`, `Classical.choice` and `Quot.sound`. [Lean proof](CellsProofs/Transport/OpenChannelBlock.lean) · [Native blocker](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/f8c6c32665c1ba8c0bcd8b687e4a7f961dc1cc5e/src/cells/laws/channels/open_channel_block.py#L69-L107)

On frozen main `f8c6c326`, the compiled CPU float64 clamp for `resolved_pump_tissue@3` and its `resolved_pump_composite@1` wrapper exercised all 18 synthetic blocker cases. Each used the 361 declared voltages from −120 to +60 mV plus the reversal and its two 0.5 mV neighbours wherever they add an in-range point. Across the 6,543 case–voltage combinations the largest relative errors were below 8 × 10⁻¹⁵ for the free fraction and 1.2 × 10⁻¹⁴ for the absolute blocker slope. The full-current derivative stayed within the stage bound. A separate four-mode automatic-differentiation control at 37 voltages agreed within 2 × 10⁻¹³, including a zero-steepness mode and a weight sum two ulps above one. Fifteen native controls passed, including compiled zero and strong block. Run `JAX_PLATFORMS=cpu JAX_ENABLE_X64=1 python -B checks/weighted_channel_block.py` in `lean/` with the pinned engine and its `src/` on `PYTHONPATH`. [Replay](checks/weighted_channel_block.py) · [Decimal reference](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/f8c6c32665c1ba8c0bcd8b687e4a7f961dc1cc5e/validation/fr51/closed_forms.py#L52-L105)

Two independent nodal sodium-channel studies found that extracellular protons blocked less at more positive voltages. Woodhull reported about 60% and 20% reductions in sodium permeability at +20 and +180 mV when bath pH fell from 7 to 5. The latter voltage exceeds the native clamp range. Mozhayeva and colleagues used instantaneous currents at pH 4.8–4.9. An independent ventricular single-channel study resolved no voltage dependence between −70 and −20 mV. The nodal pair supports the direction in that preparation. None of these measurements identifies a shared set of mode weights or tests the derivative bound quantitatively. [Woodhull, Fig. 4A](https://doi.org/10.1085/jgp.61.6.687) · [Mozhayeva et al.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/6087908/) · [Zhang and Siegelbaum, Fig. 3B](https://doi.org/10.1085/jgp.98.6.1065) · [Measurement record](checks/data/nodal_proton_block.json)

<a id="fr51-panama-holdouts"></a>

### Panama external potassium · registration stopped under §3

**D391, 26 November 2026.** The lead stopped the comparison when the registered identification check could not distinguish the 10 mM trace. Panel E shows two strokes below the circled 10, and the legend describes the overlapping fit and measurement in panel D only. Neither reader started. The 10 and 20 mM curves remain unread and unscored. The drafted erratum was unused. The §2 engine check passed. The external-potassium composition keeps its development-only status, and the internal-potassium arm was exploratory. [D391 decision](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/9b6191bdf0800d70dcae81107378401ab3c8b474/docs/DECISIONS.md#L13254-L13266) · [Tagged registration, §3](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/28e8e73473e59a1b9cf96a3a773446057c8ba56c/validation/fr51_two_potassium/REGISTRATION.md#L46-L56) · [Figure and legend](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC1796793/#fig03)

The registration used FR-51 engine `955765ef` with the potassium leak and internal spermine block. Its two models shared the 5.4 mM calibration, 145 mM internal potassium, 300 K and about 7 µM free blocker. Each used the same two modes, keeping their weights, midpoint offsets and effective valences fixed. Search had previously seen the published four-level fit and intermediate reversals. The earlier 5.4 to 60 mM comparison remains exploratory. [Pinned protocol and predictions](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/28e8e73473e59a1b9cf96a3a773446057c8ba56c/validation/fr51_two_potassium/REGISTRATION.md) · [Prior exposure, D356](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/f8c6c32665c1ba8c0bcd8b687e4a7f961dc1cc5e/docs/https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/9b6191bdf0800d70dcae81107378401ab3c8b474/docs/DECISIONS.mdS.md#L11605-L11616) · [Exploratory results](#fr51-two-potassium)

Two readers recorded relative currents of 0.76, 0.54, 0.33 and 0.21 at −80, −75, −70 and −65 mV in the 5.4 mM calibration trace. Linear interpolation gave a half-current voltage of **−74.05 mV** within **[−75, −70] mV**. At 10 and 20 mM the concentration-based model predicted shifts of **+15.93 and +33.85 mV**. The absolute-voltage model predicted zero. The scorer was exercised on synthetic curves only. [Frozen calibration](checks/data/panama2006_external_k_anchor.json) · [Scoring code](checks/panama_holdout_shift.py)

<a id="fr44-label-wash"></a>

## 24 November 2026 · Tracer collected and retained in a compartment wash

For a well-mixed pool of amount $n$, withdrawal $r$, supply $a$, and initial label share $s$, the label contributes $rs$ to the sample and keeps $(n-r)s$. Only the unlabelled base receives $a$. Lean proves the balance for each label and that the resulting shares sum to one. A whole wash with positive refill leaves only the base. If the pool is emptied without refill, its stored shares carry zero physical amount. For equal carrier concentrations in the old liquid and refill, an old label is diluted by the retained volume fraction. The six theorems use the pinned Mathlib and report only `propext`, `Classical.choice` and `Quot.sound`. [Wash proofs](CellsProofs/Tissue/CompartmentWash.lean) · [Engine exchange](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/3552e6cd19ddd1b3c12f709dd48df24b7a9aba6b/src/cells/numerics/label_shares.py#L159-L182)

On frozen main `3552e6cd`, `resolved_pump_composite@1` with `resolved_pump_tissue@3`, `neutral_arrival_labels@1` and `scheduled_compartment_wash@1` ran four isolated wells in one CPU float64 run. Each well started with 1 fmol of tagged carrier. Each refill supplied the same concentration of unlabelled carrier during an open tagging window. The largest discrepancy in either label’s inventory was about 4.44 × 10⁻¹⁶ fmol over twelve exchanges. The nine exchange controls and the compiled labelled/unlabelled twin test also passed. The twin tests cover complete exchange, cumulative samples and unchanged physical histories. [Compiled assay replay](checks/label_wash.py) · [Engine twin test](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/3552e6cd19ddd1b3c12f709dd48df24b7a9aba6b/tests/unit/test_neutral_labels_events.py#L163)

| Experimental readout | Observed | Cells |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Vloemans, diluted plug / undiluted diverted stock, at blank fractions 0.5, 0.8 and 0.9 of the final plug | 0.498, 0.193, 0.093 | 0.500, 0.200, 0.100 |
| Stubbington, fluorescence remaining in the same chamber after 1, 5 and 9 exchanges of 20% | ≈0.90, 0.21 and 0.05 | 0.800, 0.328 and 0.134 |

Vloemans assayed aliquots of both branches and reported wall adsorption. The metered part was diluted downstream, and no total recovery for the split was measured. Stubbington imaged one focal plane after equilibration, with SEM across three repeats. The withdrawal was unassayed. At wash five the plotted intensity is about 0.21 ± 0.06 SEM. Late signals fall below ideal dilution, and the author discusses possible fluorescence nonlinearity. Figure 3.6 has data markers through wash nine. The reference line reaches wash ten and follows the stated exchange fraction, while printed Eq. 3.7 would rise. Both experiments support the direction of dilution. The same-wash balance of measured withdrawn and remaining tracer stays open. [Vloemans, Fig. 4A](https://doi.org/10.1039/D4LC00195H) · [Stubbington, Fig. 3.6](https://etheses.durham.ac.uk/id/eprint/12771/1/ThesisLS_final.pdf#page=61)

Run `JAX_PLATFORMS=cpu JAX_ENABLE_X64=1 python -B checks/label_wash.py` from `lean/` with the tested engine and its `src/` on `PYTHONPATH`. It reports each measured signal, predicted concentration ratio, residual and maximum label-balance error. [Chip concentrations](checks/data/vloemans2024_dilution.json) · [Chamber readings](checks/data/stubbington2018_wash.json)

<a id="fr51-two-potassium"></a>

## 24 November 2026 · Does a Kir2.1 block curve transfer to a second potassium level?

In two independent laboratories, changing external K moved the voltage at half of the conductance reference by about 0.97–0.98 mV per mV of measured reversal shift. Each preparation supplied its own spermine concentration and calibration curve. The replay used FR-51's development engine at `7ca7c9c1` with `resolved_pump_tissue@3`, `kir2p1_mh2@1` and `open_channel_block_weighted_modes@1`. These are non-blind exploratory results for the later two-arm registration. [Decision D353](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/bc11ee34852c582b7c96d260eb8bc1f31a969f48/docs/DECISIONS.md#L11467)

| External K in mM, fit → evaluation | Observed half-conductance shift (mV) | Measured reversal shift (mV) | Cells concentration-based shift (mV) | RMS fraction error with measured shift | RMS with Cells shift | RMS with absolute voltage | Scored voltages |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| Panama, 5.4 → 60 | +51.6 | +53.2 | +62.3 | 0.044 | 0.226 | 0.397 | 9 |
| Ishihara, 150 → 50 | −26.1 | −26.9 | −28.2 | 0.010 | 0.026 | 0.479 | 10 |
| Ishihara, 150 → 20 | −48.1 | −48.9 | −51.7 | 0.015 | 0.043 | 0.533 | 10 |

The shifts in column two come from interpolating the two graph readings on either side of **half the total normalized conductance**. They give an external slope of about 0.97 for Panama and 0.97 and 0.98 for Ishihara. The two Ishihara ratios share one calibration. Panama separately reports 0.98 ± 0.01 for a fitted **steep component** across four K levels in two patches. Individual patch curves and their covariance are unavailable. Panama's representative zero crossings and relative-current curves are in different panels with unspecified specimen linkage. Ishihara reports mean reversals and uses separate patches at each K, with five at 150 mM, six to eight at 50 mM and two at 20 mM. The last group lacks reported statistical errors. Representative reading precision is about 1 mV horizontally. The ten-millivolt brackets around some half crossings limit claims about the exact slope. [Panama and Lopatin, Fig. 3 and Table 2](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC1796793/#fig03) · [Ishihara and Yan, Figs. 4–5](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/17640933/)

Each fit uses equally weighted normalized fractions from the first K level and two positive-slope blocking modes with effective valences within the engine's limit of eight. The two modes describe the training curve. Their fitted weights and valences lack independent identification. At the held-out K, the compiled clamp divides a blocked population's current by a matched free population's current on the same patch, with their Kir gates fixed equal. The absolute-voltage control uses the native membrane-voltage reference. To test the measured reversal shift, a diagnostic offsets the clamp command by the difference between the calculated and measured shifts. The two-mode parameters, declared dose and paired populations stay fixed throughout evaluation. Linear interpolation of the training curve gives similar errors. Each arm uses the same retained points, within the training support of both shifts and outside a 2 mV band around either reversal. The papers estimate the unblocked scale from negative-voltage current or a low-dose maximum. This comparison scores normalized conductance. Absolute current, blocker occupancy and a strict statistical equivalence margin remain open. [Graph readings and preparation details](checks/data/kir_spermine_potassium.json) · [Compiled comparison and all pointwise results](checks/kir_spermine_potassium.py)

**Internal potassium gives a different result.** With external K at 150 mM and internal spermine at 20 µM, raising internal K from 150 to 625 mM shifts the plotted half-block voltage from about −20 to −15 mV. The paper's plotted K reversal changes by about −36 mV. The corresponding FR-51 predictions are a −36.3 mV shift with a K reference and zero with an absolute-voltage reference. Both native controls reach a blocked/free current ratio of 0.5 at their predicted midpoint. The full experimental series has a fitted slope of −0.15. This is one laboratory, and the Fig. 3C group means have one to three patches per K level. Figs. 3A–B show a same-patch change. [Lopatin and Nichols, Fig. 3](https://doi.org/10.1085/jgp.108.2.105)

Lean establishes exact translation for any fixed block fraction depending on V − E when the unblocked current is nonzero in each solution. It uses Mathlib's cancellation lemmas, with `propext`, `Classical.choice` and `Quot.sound` in the real-number specialization. The paper comparison supplies the test of that fixed-fraction assumption. To reproduce the 24 November record, run `JAX_ENABLE_X64=1 JAX_PLATFORMS=cpu PYTHONPATH="$ENGINE/src:$ENGINE" python -B checks/kir_spermine_potassium.py` from `lean/` at driver revision `369431f7`, with `ENGINE` pointing to `7ca7c9c1`. [Lean theorem](CellsProofs/Transport/IonChannels.lean#L333) · [FR-64 replay](#fr64-block-replays)

<a id="red-cell-chloride-ratio"></a>

## 24 November 2026 · Can the initial chloride ratio explain the red-cell current mismatch?

At the two mean voltages in Freedman and Novak's Fig. 6, a common constant-field permeability can fit both mean currents if intracellular chloride is **28.1 mM** with external chloride at 150 mM. The required ratio is **0.187**. Table I's initial ratio was **0.67 ± 0.02** (donor SD). Across ratios 0.65–0.69, the −103 mV calibration predicts −1.96 to −1.98 mA/g Hb at −59 mV, compared with −1.17 ± 0.13. Varying both graph voltages and currents over their digitization precision gives ratios 0.161–0.222. These ranges describe input sensitivity. The paper gives no paired intracellular chloride measurement at each voltage or donor-level covariance for these means. [Freedman and Novak, Table I and Figs. 5–6](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC2220065/) · [Two-current inversion](checks/red_cell_chloride_ratio.py)

Fig. 5A and C reuse the same five series. Fig. 6 groups those observations by external K. Two series also appeared in the authors' 1994 paper. An independent human red-cell study measured net chloride efflux under valinomycin with tracer of matched specific activity. Its voltage series used 0.7 mM external chloride, a K-gradient estimate of voltage and the inhibitor DNDS. It supplies a distinct condition and cannot replicate the 150 mM DIDS residual-current curve. The single-permeability discrepancy stays a scope finding under D351. [Fröhlich, Leibson and Gunn, Fig. 6](https://doi.org/10.1085/jgp.81.1.127) · [Cells decision D351](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/8eb9bc8b032fbbabe446e073a65cbd9b32fe1729/docs/DECISIONS.md#L11377)

| Observable | Result with the preparation’s own inputs | What the measurement resolves |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Open Kir conductance in low external K | For the Na-free Fig. 4 solutions, Ishihara’s Table 2 gives 21.3 and 48.4 mM total K against 149.1 mM. The native slope fractions are 0.378 and 0.570. The figure gives about 0.83 and 0.90. Matsuda’s independent 30/150 mM series gives 0.729 against 0.447. | This strengthens the existing comparison. The first series is cloned mouse Kir2.1. The ventricular channel in the second was untyped. Activity and whole-patch chord conductance are separate from the model’s concentration input and the unitary slope readout. [Ishihara](https://doi.org/10.1085/jgp.201711936) · [Matsuda](https://doi.org/10.1113/jphysiol.1991.sp018499) |

Panama’s steep-component fit gives 5.0 ± 0.2 (SEM, seven patches), or 5.17 mV per e-fold at the authors’ 300 K. | A proposed blocker-only model with charge +4 and electrical distance at most one cannot reach either fitted central slope. The fits do not count displaced potassium ions. An internal-K series also shifts the block differently from an external-K series. FR-51 is still a proposed block law. [Ishihara, Fig. 3](https://doi.org/10.1113/jphysiol.2003.055434) · [Panama, Table 1](https://doi.org/10.1113/jphysiol.2005.097741) · [Lopatin, Figs. 2–3](https://doi.org/10.1085/jgp.108.2.105) |

Nominal roots over the recording temperatures are +58.56 to +58.96, zero and −58.96 to −58.56 mV. | These two datasets are whole-cell recordings. They resolve direction and approximate scale. Sasaki independently found a near-proton-equilibrium reversal. A three-lab, excised-patch slope with measured face pH and uncertainties remains open. The same-lab mitoplast comparison keeps its own verdict. [Cherny, Fig. 2](https://doi.org/10.1085/jgp.105.6.861) · [Ramsey, Fig. 1](https://doi.org/10.1038/nature04700) · [Sasaki](https://doi.org/10.1126/science.1122352) |
| Vacuolar pump current at fixed cytosolic pH | Lumen acidification shifted the yeast ATP-dependent current reversal by +13.0 mV against +112.4 mV for tight coupling. In the plant, the first measured reversal, −94.5 mV, fixes the energy offset. At lumen pH 6.5 and 5.5 Cells gives −36.3 to −35.3 and +21.8 to +23.8 mV at 20–25 °C. The measurements are −67.5 ± 10.6 and −49.6 ± 3.9 mV. | The plant offsets agree across all four tested fixed stoichiometries. The plant experiment lacks a measured nucleotide activity quotient for the ramps. The Arabidopsis data reinforce the FR-29 gradient finding. Yeast lumen pH 3.1 and actual proton/ATP counts remain separate targets. Davies’ beet data are original in the 1994 paper and replotted in Kettner Fig. 4. [Yeast, Table 2](https://doi.org/10.1016/S0006-3495(03)74789-4) · [Arabidopsis, Fig. 2](https://doi.org/10.1074/jbc.M111.310367) · [Red beet](https://doi.org/10.1073/pnas.91.18.8547) |
| Myoglobin oxygen occupancy | At 10 °C and an apparent human P50 of 0.3311 Torr from two points, three other readings gave RMSE 0.0422 with the native rapid-binding form weights. The equine fit at 37 °C and pH 7.0 gave 2.39 Torr. Cells gives half occupancy when supplied that fitted P50. | The independent observations support the approximate isotherm. A common affinity across species or temperatures and a second fit-free pointwise test remain open. Tyuma’s cited titration concerns hemoglobin. [Human myoglobin](https://doi.org/10.1016/0003-9861(58)90094-8) · [Equine myoglobin](https://doi.org/10.1152/jappl.1997.82.1.86) · [Tyuma](https://doi.org/10.1021/bi00732a004) |

Run `JAX_PLATFORMS=cpu JAX_ENABLE_X64=1 python -B checks/paired_preparation_controls.py` from `lean/` in the Cells environment. The Kir slope, proton reversal, tight-coupled pump and binding controls call native laws. For the existing tissue Kir2.1 population, halving prescribed availability gave a current ratio of exactly 0.5 at −20, −10, +10 and +20 mV with gates held fixed. This control establishes how the present input acts. The two spermine valences come from experimental fits. [Driver](checks/paired_preparation_controls.py) · [Measurement inputs and primary locators](checks/data/paired_preparation_controls.json) · [Paired verdicts](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md)

The two perfused squid series support depolarization with external K. Baker varied the internal salt and some external ions. Ohki supplied the better matched within-preparation substitution series. For K–Cl cotransport, Brugnara’s human cells show chloride driving uphill K efflux. That observation leaves the nominal concentration-product null in sheep open. In cardiac cells with Cs replacing K, both the calculated −24 mV pump reversal and the authors’ approximate −60 mV fell in a broad unresolved region. The squid and cardiac studies both support inward pump current under reverse-favouring conditions. The red-cell ghost studies measure phosphate label entering ATP. [Baker, Fig. 2](https://doi.org/10.1113/jphysiol.1962.sp007026) · [Ohki, Table 1](https://doi.org/10.2170/jjphysiol.29.373) · [Brugnara](https://doi.org/10.1152/ajpcell.1989.256.5.C994) · [Squid pump current](https://doi.org/10.1038/309450a0) · [Cardiac current, Fig. 5](https://doi.org/10.1113/jphysiol.1995.sp020690) · [Cardiac forward and reverse solutions](https://doi.org/10.1073/pnas.85.10.3412)

Lamin B1 half-lives of 4.4 and 8.40 days came from hippocampal and cortical rat cultures using different labels and readouts. In adult mouse cortex the source workbook gives 46.98 days in homogenate. For Bassoon, Dörrbaum Fig. 2C reuses tom Dieck’s images. Tsuriel’s independent local photoactivation resolves export to nearby boutons, while whole-neuron loss remains open. For RNA, Pendleton and Gasser measured continued precursor incorporation after an external label switch. Neither experiment measures survival of a cohort identified at template release. [Dörrbaum, Fig. 2](https://doi.org/10.7554/eLife.34202) · [Heo, Table 2](https://doi.org/10.1073/pnas.1720956115) · [Fornasiero, Supplementary Data 1](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41467-018-06519-0) · [tom Dieck](https://doi.org/10.1038/nmeth.3319) · [Tsuriel, Fig. 5](https://doi.org/10.1523/JNEUROSCI.4777-08.2009) · [Pendleton, Fig. 2](https://rnajournal.cshlp.org/content/24/6/778.full) · [Gasser, Fig. 5](https://doi.org/10.1002/anie.201916272)

Three proposed quantitative tests still lack a matched measurement. The ATP thermodynamic reconstructions use earlier reaction values and different Mg conventions. The full product of independently measured constants around one cycle remains open. Lagoy’s last bar is after **four** wash wells and a fill delay. Li’s fifth point is drained rinse liquid. A common fifth-rinse prediction for retained liquid would compare different quantities. Olbrich’s ramp lysis tensions at 21 °C supply neither a second temperature-resolved DOPC hold rate nor the time of first pore opening in the same vesicle. [ATP glutamine route](https://doi.org/10.1042/bj0710400) · [ATP acetyl route](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/4355193/) · [Lagoy, Fig. 1i](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41598-018-24504-x) · [Li, Fig. S-6A](https://doi.org/10.1039/C6LC01201A) · [Olbrich](https://doi.org/10.1016/S0006-3495(00)76294-1)

Mathieson’s neuronal isotope data provide a separate test of the ordering of two proteins. GM130 and Lamin B1 had fitted half-lives of 1.9 and 4.4 days in Dörrbaum’s rat neuronal culture. In Mathieson’s two mouse neuronal replicates, GM130 was 1.46 and 1.68 days, while Lamin B1 was 7.04 and 8.71 days. The much longer Lamin B1 estimates in the first columns of the same worksheet belong to B cells. Dörrbaum, Kochen, Langer and Schuman authored the rat study. The author lists are disjoint, and the verified mouse culture has its own raw mass-spectrometry accession. [Dörrbaum, Fig. 2B](https://doi.org/10.7554/eLife.34202) · [Mathieson, Supplementary Data 2 and exact cells](checks/data/mathieson2018_neuronal_turnover.json) · [Mouse neuron raw data](https://www.ebi.ac.uk/pride/archive/projects/PXD008514)

## 24 November 2026 · Which replicated observations does Cells reproduce?

All 46 previously paired observations have a Cells assessment. Seven additional comparisons bring the register to 53. This first pass flagged eight replay discrepancies for review, including Cx43, SK2, POPC and the deterministic vesicle rupture threshold. Each row names the observable, the two experiments and the scope of the replay. [Replay results and D351’s subsequent ruling](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md#cells-replay-results)

| Preparation and test | Cells result | Measurement and interpretation |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Red-cell chloride, calibrate at −103 mV and evaluate at −59 mV | −1.97 mA/g Hb, or −2.09 to −1.86 over the calibration and voltage figure-reading allowances | −1.17 ± 0.13 mA/g Hb (SD). The measured conductance-slope ratio is 3.67 against an upper bound of 1.49 for this constant-field preparation. The indirect current and voltage estimates lack donor-level covariance. The authors compared barrier and gating models. [Freedman and Novak](https://doi.org/10.1085/jgp.109.2.201) |
| Valinomycin bilayer K, calibrate at +110 mV and evaluate at −110 mV in the same light state | −3.84 nA, spanning −3.88 to −3.80 for 20–30 °C | About −13.5 nA, a figure reading without a replicate error. The local reversal is close. Matching the second current would require 3.52 times the fitted permeability, versus 0.59 times for red-cell chloride. Piasta’s independent bilayer points cover roughly 2 mV near reversal. A wide-range replication of the carrier shape remains open. [Pfeffermann, Fig. 2c](https://doi.org/10.1039/D5NR04234H) · [Piasta, Fig. B1](https://doi.org/10.1085/jgp.201110684) |
| Kv1.5 activation midpoint, independent acid/control baths with 10 and 4 mM K | Zero midpoint shift within 10⁻¹² mV in both normalized native tail series, including Longobardo’s different tail voltages | +11.7 and +17.3 mV. The registered gate reads voltage. A common scale within each curve cancels on normalization. Absolute activation curves depend on the pulse and tail measurement method. [Trapani and Korn](https://doi.org/10.1016/S0006-3495(03)74842-5) · [Longobardo et al.](https://doi.org/10.1038/sj.bjp.0704251) |
| Kv1.5 potassium–pH interaction, tested in the voltage domain | At +40 mV the low/4.5 mM K peak-current ratio approached 1.008 at both pH values. The fitted acid/control tail maxima at 3.5 and 140 mM K both gave 1.000 | Jäger measured near 1 and about 0.1 at nominal zero K, at pH 7.4 and 6.0. Kehl measured 0.56 ± 0.01 and 0.81 ± 0.12 for acid/control at fixed K. The second comparison uses Kehl’s complete −40 to +55 mV grid at 140 mM and the overlapping −30 to +45 mV grid at 3.5 mM. The +65 mV acidic endpoint and Trapani’s +100 mV command fall outside the law. [Jäger, Fig. 2](https://doi.org/10.1016/S0014-5793(00)02396-6) · [Kehl, Fig. 2](https://doi.org/10.1113/jphysiol.2001.014456) |
| Inward unitary conductance after lowering K without Na substitution | A high-K anchor gives 15.3 pS for 150→20 mM and 14.2 pS for 150→30 mM. At equal driving voltage the native square-root slope ratios do not depend on the common rectification parameters. | Mouse Kir2.1 gave about 35 pS and native guinea-pig ventricular channels gave 23.1 ± 1.2 pS (SD). With Na replacing K, Ishihara instead found about 18.5 pS at 20 mM K. These are slopes of nearly linear inward I–V segments. The ventricular subtype and an exact refit over each patch’s voltage window remain open. D340’s separate Kir4.1 series still differ. [Ishihara, Fig. 4](https://doi.org/10.1085/jgp.201711936) · [Matsuda, Figs. 1–2](https://doi.org/10.1113/jphysiol.1991.sp018499) |

The pH replays change the actual extracellular free-H stock while preserving compartment charge and leaving the declared channel availability fixed. The host requires positive concentrations. Jäger’s nominally zero-K condition is approached at 0.001 and 0.000001 mM. Cells uses its declared gate kinetics at 298.15 K. The pipette salt concentrations used for driving force exclude unreported KOH corrections. The paired protocol tests use normalized quantities. [Clamp driver](checks/paired_law_replays.py) · [Public-paper inputs and limitations](checks/data/paired_replay_measurements.json)

Two native surface populations with each study’s measured weights and lifetimes reproduce their fitted receptor survival curves. The seven-day totals are 0.602 and 0.541 relative to their respective fit origins. The volume replay for Viallat gives 0.309 against about 0.314 measured, alongside 0.246 against about 0.25 for Fujiwara. At the POPC temperatures tested by both groups, a single measured cold footprint gives warm/cold area 1.000. At constant tension the deterministic comparator cannot produce continuing loss. The alternative FR-31 mechanism covers tension holds. A common rate law across the DOPC ramp histograms remains open. [Receptor replays](checks/paired_law_replays.py) · [Volumetric and threshold controls](checks/paired_response_endpoints.py)

Two independent groups recovered albumin after washing expanded PTFE. The complete native draw retained all 10 fmol initially on a synthetic wall. A material-specific release curve still needs starting surface stock and timed wash fractions. In red-cell ghosts two groups found gradient-dependent, ouabain-sensitive phosphate labelling of ATP. Signed net pump turnover cannot be compared directly to the tracer signal because gross exchange contributes to labelling. Passive droplet split images from separate laboratories also show particles in both daughters. Fornell and Liu share investigators. Their quantitative estimates of liquid volume and bead count are unpaired. The field-off Sahore image establishes occupancy, while its reported 59% withdrawal volume belongs to the magnetic experiment. <a id="paired-membrane-physiology"></a>

## 24 November 2026 · Reversal voltages, junction timing and receptor turnover

Hodgkin and Katz’s intact squid axons depolarized by 2 and 4 mV when bath potassium replaced sodium at 15 and 20 mM K, starting at 10 mM. In Ohki and Aono’s independent perfused axons, starting at 10 mM K gave +2.0 mV at 30 mM and +19.8 mV at 100 mM (six axons). Transferring the 1949 permeability ratios to the nominal 500 mM internal K perfusate gave +12.63 and +34.50 mV in the native constant-field flux calculation. That projection treats the nominal internal sodium and chloride as zero and omits fluoride, phosphate and calcium currents. A second comparison uses only the 1979 preparation. At fixed internal composition and fixed external chloride, exp(VF/RT) is linear in the amount of external K replacing Na. Anchoring at 10 and 350 mM, the errors at 30, 50, 100 and 200 mM were +3.76, +3.25, −0.42 and −0.83 mV. The table supplies no covariance between bath measurements. The ionic currents were unmeasured. [Squid studies](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md#pair-squid-potassium-substitution) · [Ohki and Aono, Table 1a](https://www.jstage.jst.go.jp/article/jjphysiol1950/29/4/29_4_373/_pdf/-char/ja#page=6)

Two independent laboratories measured proton-current reversal from tail currents. At bath pH 7.5, changing macrophage pipette pH from 6.8 to 6.0 moved the measured mean from −38.07 to −69.17 mV. The nominal native-law roots at 22 °C were −40.99 and −87.85 mV. In microglia at pipette pH 5.5, the measured reversal moved from about −31 to −86 mV across bath pH 6.3–7.8. The nominal roots at the midpoint recording temperature of 23 °C were −47.01 and −135.15 mV. The macrophage study separately measured intracellular alkalinization during long pulses. Tail-time intracellular pH remains unknown in the microglial series. Direct brown-fat and skeletal-muscle mitoplast measurements were closer to the expected one-pH-unit shift, and both came from the same laboratory. [Independent tail recordings](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md#pair-proton-gradient-tail-reversal) · [Mitoplast evidence and provenance](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md#pair-mitoplast-proton-reversal-independence)

At the same sustained −100 mV step, wild-type Cx43 currents decayed on two time scales in independent oocyte and N2a recordings. The time constants were 417 ms and 7.06 s in oocytes, and 70 ms and 931 ms in N2a pairs. We marked the single-state junction claim’s biological evidence **mixed**. It captures voltage-dependent relaxation, and both preparations resolve a second phase. A third laboratory measured 80 ± 3.8% fast and 20 ± 3.8% slow initial amplitudes at 110 mV in seven untagged Cx43 pairs. Two-component fits leave the two-gate law’s mixed amplitude unmeasured. [Single gate](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md#pair-cx43-single-exponential-clamp) · [Two-gate amplitude test](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md#pair-cx43-series-mixed-amplitude)

Two innervated mouse muscle studies found fast acetylcholine-receptor half-lives near one day and slow half-lives of 12–13 days. Independent low-occupancy labelling also followed the decline of a receptor cohort. In human adult muscle, an endplate preparation with 44.5% of the control binding-site count retained 46.3% of the control miniature current. Lizard site-density measurements support the same direction. Independent muscle and neuronal studies both detected previously exposed receptors returning to the surface. Neither measured the terminal internal receptor stock and returned area needed for a closed recycling balance. [Cohort loss](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md#pair-innervated-receptor-cohort-loss) · [Lifetime mixture](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md#pair-innervated-receptor-single-rate) · [Binding sites and current](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md#pair-receptor-sites-and-current) · [Recycling](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md#pair-receptor-return-to-surface)

Run `python -B lean/checks/paired_membrane_physiology.py` in the Cells CPU environment with the checkout root and `src` on `PYTHONPATH`. The check finds zeroes of the compiled ion fluxes and compares them with the analytical reversals. The arbitrary positive area and permeability scale cancel from the zero. These are flux-law checks. The data supply resting voltages and tail-current reversals without a calibration of absolute permeability. [Driver](checks/paired_membrane_physiology.py) · [Measurements, uncertainties and provenance](checks/data/membrane_physiology_pairs.json)

## 24 November 2026 · Calcium buffering, membrane footprints and channel calibration

For the rapid-binding law, Lean proves that increasing free ligand from x to y at fixed binder totals and other free ligands requires a total-ligand increase of at least y − x. The proof uses Mathlib’s derivative criterion for monotonicity and the nonnegative variance of each binder’s ligand count. A compiled CPU check of the conservation residual gave total/free increment ratios of 1 with no binder. With 2 mM of a synthetic single-site buffer and Kd = 1 mM, all four ratios agreed with the analytical values, from 2.067 down to 1.133. Two independent albumin experiments measured both total and ionized calcium. The endpoint ratios were 1.98 and 1.37, and all four adjacent steps of the first series satisfied the bound. The experiments differ in albumin, solution and Mg control. An ATP-specific proton response still needs a different measurement. [Proof](CellsProofs/Chemistry/BindingResponse.lean) · [Compiled check and measured increments](checks/binding_buffer_capacity.py) · [Albumin pair](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md#pair-albumin-calcium-buffering)

For two spherical headgroup surfaces, Lean shows that equality of molecular footprints is equivalent to equality between the measured inner lipid fraction and the inner surface-area fraction. Finer measured 28% inner lipid versus 26.47% from geometry. Huang and Mason inferred 32.26% versus 28.17%. Both residuals have the same sign. The authors’ interpretations differ and the geometric inputs share a source lineage. In fluid POPC, two independent methods found that the area per molecule increases with temperature. A closed 64-lipid Cells vesicle gave 20.576 nm² at both 30 and 50 °C with the 30 °C footprint. Using the 50 °C footprint at 50 °C gave 21.536 nm². These are compiled geometry outputs with experimentally supplied inputs. An independent whole-vesicle area and lipid count remain open. [Proof](CellsProofs/Chemistry/Vesicle.lean) · [Native replay](checks/leaflet_packing.py) · [Leaflet experiments](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md#pair-egg-pc-leaflet-packing) · [Temperature series](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md#pair-popc-temperature-footprint)

Acidification shifted human Kv1.5 activation by +11.7 and +17.3 mV in two labs. Strong-depolarization measurements also showed that external potassium protects the available current. For Kir4.1, Tada reported an inward-conductance exponent of 0.51, while Edvinsson’s series reached approximately 19 pS at 40 mM against 13.9 pS from a square root anchored at 110 mM. These are inward slopes. The current at a fixed driving voltage has its own comparison. A separate retinal-glia recording supports the direction of the K-dependent reversal shift. [Kv1.5 voltage](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md#pair-kv15-acid-activation-shift) · [Kv1.5 current](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md#pair-kv15-potassium-protection) · [Kir4.1 conductance](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md#pair-kir41-slope-scaling) · [Reversal](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md#pair-kir-potassium-reversal)

Two Mg–ATP measurements at pH 7.2 and 37 °C gave apparent dissociation constants of 87.4 and 27.7 µM in different media. Two vesicle-insertion studies leave the radius-versus-area scaling unresolved because neither supplies absolute molecule flux across known sizes under matched supply. [Mg–ATP](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md#pair-mgatp-apparent-affinity) · [Vesicle insertion](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md#pair-vesicle-insertion-size-exponent)

Run `lake build` from `lean/`. Both new declarations report only `propext`, `Classical.choice` and `Quot.sound` under `#print axioms`. For the CPU replays, run `python -B lean/checks/binding_buffer_capacity.py` and `python -B lean/checks/leaflet_packing.py` with the Cells CPU environment and the checkout’s root and `src` on `PYTHONPATH`. Inputs, methods, units and uncertainties are recorded alongside the checks. ## 24 November 2026 · Channel timing, membrane transport and rupture

For the held-input gate, the time taken to rise from 20% to 80% of its excursion equals ln(4)/(a + b), where a and b are the opening and closing rates. It is bounded by ln(4) times the zero-input washout time constant whenever b is positive and shared. The Lean proof follows the actual state trajectory and uses the pinned Mathlib exponential and logarithm. The axiom audit reported `propext`, `Classical.choice` and `Quot.sound`. In a compiled held-dose world with a = b = 16/s and initial state 0.125, both versions of `species_regulated_availability` bracketed the interval at 42.97–43.95 ms on a 0.488 ms grid. The exact value is 43.32 ms. [Lean proof](CellsProofs/Transport/RegulatedAvailability.lean) · [Reproducible CPU comparison](checks/paired_transport.py) · [Measured inputs](checks/data/paired_transport_responses.json)

Syme’s independent untreated rat SK2 dose response gives half-activation at 0.700 µM calcium. The Hirschberg-calibrated Cells curve gives 0.728 µM and reaches 47.45% of its own ceiling at 0.700 µM. For kinetics, Hirschberg’s single-channel plateau and macropatch washout predict a 10.63 ms activation time constant against 4.1 ± 0.6 ms measured. Oliver’s separate recombinant SK2 experiments report a 34.7 ± 6.5 ms mean washout and a 6.18 ± 1.91 ms 20–80% rise at 10 µM. Reusing that washout at 0.5 µM gives a 48.10 ms ceiling against a reported rise of about 80 ms. The low-dose result lacks an error bar and a paired washout. [Steady response](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md#pair-rsk2-calcium-midpoint) · [Closing-rate comparison](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md#pair-rsk2-single-closing-rate)

Two epithelial chloride preparations agree on the sign of the reversal shift. Constant-field current magnitudes show larger discrepancies in red cells and a planar potassium bilayer when voltage changes. Separate DOPC hold and ramp experiments also show that rupture depends on time spent under tension. These comparisons concern the preparations in the linked records. The rupture observations constrain the fixed-threshold law; a separate Cells model already has a time-dependent pore hazard. [Chloride reversal](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md#pair-epithelial-chloride-gradient) · [Current magnitudes](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md#pair-single-permeability-current-voltage) · [Rupture timing](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md#pair-dopc-rupture-time-history) · [Kinetic pore model](claims/vesicle-pore-rate.md)

Independent wet-lab data also support near-1:1 red-cell K–Cl coupling and preferential cholesterol partition into SOPC over DOPC. The neuronal K–Cl reversal is inferred from voltage. The two sheep red-cell null measurements came from the same laboratory under different volume and pH protocols. The cholesterol crystal measurements used feed composition and different preparation methods. [K–Cl stoichiometry](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md#pair-erythrocyte-kcl-stoichiometry) · [Sheep flux nulls](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md#pair-kcl-null-across-sheep-protocols) · [Equilibrium cholesterol partition](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md#pair-cholesterol-host-lipid-affinity) · [Crystal onset](WET_LAB_EVIDENCE.md#pair-dopc-crystal-onset-portability) · [Measurements](checks/data/paired_wet_lab_measurements.json)

<a id="xc0-charge-anchor"></a>

## 24 November 2026 · Resolution of the initial charge check

For `resolved_pump_tissue@3`, the XC0 builder stores a fixed background in fmol so that mobile ions plus that background give the declared charge. The definition check reads the same valence-weighted amounts and converts their sum back to coulombs. Lean bounds the difference between the reported residual and a deliberately introduced charge error. Multiply the four rounding allowances in fmol (division by the conversion factor, ionic sum, stored background and reconstructed sum) by the charge per fmol, then add the two allowances in C (multiplication and final subtraction). Call the result E. A threshold T ≥ E admits a balanced input. A true discrepancy exceeding T + E in magnitude is always detected. Mathlib supplies the nearest-grid distance bound for either choice at a tie. The per-operation hypotheses are explicit, including both sums. [Proof](CellsProofs/Tissue/ChargeAnchor.lean) · [XC0 builder](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/cd3b7b09d8252f445a976f0506767f1aaf38c53d/validation/xenopus_bioelectric/xc0_worlds.py#L164) · [Engine check](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/cd3b7b09d8252f445a976f0506767f1aaf38c53d/src/cells/models/surface_tissue.py#L421)

On frozen engine `cd3b7b09`, the public parser refused seven of 15 balanced development inputs. At 38 mM Na the first refusal in the sweep occurred with 1.5 × 2²⁰ fmol of B. At 21 mM Na it occurred with 1.5 × 2²¹ fmol. In a separate exact midpoint control, either sign left a residual of 1.123237099 × 10⁻²⁰ C, above the fixed 10⁻²⁰ C cutoff. The same midpoint one power of two lower was admitted. At 38 mM Na, moving the stored background by four binary64 spacings in either direction (±9.313225746 × 10⁻¹⁰ fmol) produced residual magnitudes of 7.94 × 10⁻²⁰ and 1.00 × 10⁻¹⁹ C. Both exceeded the 2.25 × 10⁻²⁰ C scale-aware threshold for those inputs. An exact rational check verified all six half-spacing allowances on all 17 inputs. Using T = max(10⁻²⁰ C, E) classified all 17 correctly. The driver prints the per-case JSON and exits 1 while the native decisions disagree. Run `lean-xc0-charge-resolution-20261124` records the output as `charge-anchor.json`. D333 assigns the engine repair after FR-17. The registered XC0 v1 verdict stands. [Replay](checks/charge_anchor.py) · [Diagnosis and decision](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/23bf52341b6a096dc19d69d7dac509d4f46eeb00/docs/DECISIONS.md#L10511)

After FR-17 stage 1 landed on main at `8760d5b3`, a fresh run on that frozen revision produced byte-identical JSON across all 17 cases. The charge check still uses the absolute floor. To repeat from `lean/` in the Cells environment, run `python -B checks/charge_anchor.py`. [Parser after FR-17](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/8760d5b338a1f7e9a930a8492c434efaa50815e7/src/cells/models/surface_tissue.py#L440)

Search checked what physiology can contribute. Guizouarn et al. measured Na, K, Cl and membrane voltage in control *Xenopus* oocytes with different sample counts. Their approximately 500 nL of intracellular water per oocyte came from five pooled oocytes, corrected for trapped extracellular water. A physical comparison would also need fixed charge and the corresponding capacitance and voltage for the same cell. The numerical allowance here comes from the resolution of the declared inputs. [Guizouarn et al., Table I and methods](https://doi.org/10.1074/jbc.M311920200)

<a id="fr47-shell-interval-heat"></a>

## 24 November 2026 · Free energy as tracer refills an empty pool

For `cells.rate_equations@1` and `cells.shell_permeation@1`, Lean proves that the ideal free energy of the two mean counts is nonincreasing between washes. The proof uses the fixed shell rates and each compartment’s reference capacity in molecules. Mathlib gives continuity of n ln n at zero. On the interior the flux and its log ratio give the sign of the derivative. This covers an empty pool, an empty lumen and zero total tracer. A sealed shell releases zero. The registered P9 substitution changes an empty starting place’s contribution by exactly (μ° − ln X − 1) kBT when the ending counts are positive (μ° in RT units). With unit reference counts and zero standard potentials, two retained tracers reach means (½, 1½) at t = (ln 2)/2 for unit rates. The correct heat is 4 ln 2 − (3/2) ln 3 kBT. Lean checks that subtracting one kBT still leaves it positive. [Proof](CellsProofs/Chemistry/ShellIntervalHeat.lean) · [Exact controls and axiom audit](checks/chemistry/ShellIntervalHeat.lean) · [Registered C9](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/231aae399bd9f8d5db35bc31fe95207ec664ab7b/validation/assembly_lineages_v2/fr47_spec/CRITERIA.md#L50) · [Definition of P9](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/231aae399bd9f8d5db35bc31fe95207ec664ab7b/validation/assembly_lineages_v2/fr47_spec/AMENDMENT-2026-11-24.md)

On frozen FR-47 build `43763a36`, a made-up CPU float64 world began with 240 exterior tracers. The shell took up tracer before a full wash at 0.625 s. The wash retained 18.47743757 molecules in the lumen on average. A quarter withdrawal followed at 1.75 s. All three continuous ledger amounts passed the preselected comparison rule of 10⁻⁵ times the largest predicted interval heat (0.00038347 kBT here). The largest absolute difference was 6.59 × 10⁻⁸ kBT. The instantaneous wash bookings are excluded from these intervals. The native run closed its material and dissipation checks. These are development inputs for a separate comparison from FR-47’s registered worlds. The input parameters, count reads, wash records and interval amounts are in `shell-interval-heat.json` in run `lean-fr47-interval-heat-20261124`. [Reproducible comparison](checks/shell_interval_heat.py) · [Engine interval reader](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/43763a36f27d4dcf8381770649d6a59e4747ddf5/validation/assembly_lineages_v2/fr47_judge.py#L195)

| Interval (s) | Place empty at start | Booked (kBT) | Predicted (kBT) | P9 (kBT) |
| --- | --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| 0–0.625 | Lumen | 27.89470516 | 27.89470515 | 15.90123990 |
| 0.625–1.75 | Exterior | 38.34719503 | 38.34719500 | 24.33882673 |
| 1.75–2.5 | Neither | 1.69962899 | 1.69962893 | 1.69962893 |

The evidence Search agent found no matched measurement of this free-energy drop. The existing tracer experiments measure release and redistribution. Westh derived the equilibrium free energy for transferring DMPC vesicles from water into glycerol solution and measured that transfer’s enthalpy by calorimetry. A direct test of the shell ledger could use reversible work or a chemical-potential difference measured over tracer transfer between the two compartments. [Tracer observations](claims/fr47-shell-wash-recovery.md#biological-evidence) · [Westh, Figures 3 and 5](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC1302615/)

<a id="fr47-shell-wash-recovery"></a>

## 23 November 2026 · How much tracer returns after a pool wash

**Qualification update.** The six FR-47 worlds passed their registered checks and caught all nine planted faults. The schedule supplement passed separately. D373 lifted FR-47’s development-only label. For OL6 Protocol D v2, D368 recorded a pass in all seven worlds on the first seed set. D393 lifted that protocol’s development label. Each decision applies to its registered runs. [FR-47 verdict and scope](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/9b6191bdf0800d70dcae81107378401ab3c8b474/docs/DECISIONS.md#L12337-L12362) · [Protocol D v2 verdict](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/9b6191bdf0800d70dcae81107378401ab3c8b474/docs/DECISIONS.md#L12117-L12134) · [D393 status](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/9b6191bdf0800d70dcae81107378401ab3c8b474/docs/DECISIONS.md#L13297-L13299)

For `cells.shell_permeation@1`, `cells.pool_replacement@1` and `cells.pool_withdrawal@1` on a prescribed shell, Lean derives the exterior mean flux from the two-state forward equation of each tracer. A full tracer-free replacement empties the exterior while preserving the mean lumen count L. Between washes the pool recovers as L k_out (1 − exp(−s t))/s, with s = k_in + k_out > 0. Its initial right derivative is k_out L. Mathlib’s ODE uniqueness theorem identifies this curve on the whole interval. At a later withdrawal of a fraction f on the sampler’s word grid, the expected removal is f times the recovered exterior mean. [Proof](CellsProofs/Chemistry/ShellWashRecovery.lean) · [Exact controls and axiom audit](checks/chemistry/ShellWashRecovery.lean)

On frozen engine candidate `cf5ddcff`, a synthetic one-shell example started at replacement with five exterior and seven lumen molecules. The native pool reset removed all five. Using the engine’s rates over the eight possible counts, the exterior means were 1.3607043414 at 0.75 s and 2.8910229384 at 2 s. The half-withdrawal at 2 s removed 1.4455114692 on average across all possible draw outcomes. Both reads agreed with the recovery formula within 10⁻¹² molecules. Omitting return from the exterior into the lumen gave 3.1583185473 at 2 s. The driver propagates the host rates with a matrix exponential and enumerates the threshold outcomes of the native withdrawal. The subsequent deterministic shell check used independently chosen counts and compared the continuous heat as well. [Host comparison driver](checks/shell_wash_recovery.py) · [Deterministic interval comparison](#fr47-shell-interval-heat)

Two measurements cover different parts of the prediction. After external dye removal, carboxyfluorescein release from single-bilayer DPPC vesicles had a half-time above 20 h at 37 °C. In paired finite droplets, LC–MS measured rhodamine 6G on both sides of a DOPC bilayer. The full-series apparent permeability was (6.28 ± 0.68) × 10⁻⁶ cm/s. It was fitted to those same observations. A held-out post-wash time course with known retained tracer, volumes and an independently measured permeability remains open. [Boyer and Zasadzinski, Fig. 2](https://doi.org/10.1021/nn7002025) · [Bachler et al., Fig. 5](https://doi.org/10.1021/acs.analchem.0c04939) · [Measurements and limits](checks/data/shell_tracer_recovery.json)

To reproduce, build the Lean library and run `lake env lean -DwarningAsError=true checks/chemistry/ShellWashRecovery.lean` from `lean/`. Run `python -B lean/checks/shell_wash_recovery.py` with the Cells CPU environment and the tested checkout’s `src` and root on `PYTHONPATH`. Inputs, outputs and verification logs are in the Lean run directory `lean-fr47-wash-recovery-20261123`.

## 23 November 2026 · A second ligand can carry bound X across a shell

For `cells.stochastic_mass_action@1` on `cells.prescribed_shell@1`, Lean proves that an initially empty lumen stays empty for any finite sequence of admitted events when each event balances X, every possible transfer of X stays on one face, and all inner-accessible places initially contain zero copies of X. The places include bound ligand. An initially occupied inner site can release X into an empty free lumen. The exact controls include a same-face isomerization and a release from a preloaded site. [Theorem](CellsProofs/Chemistry/ShellLigandFaces.lean) · [Exact controls](checks/chemistry/ShellLigandFaces.lean)

The development parser at `61d823be` admitted a two-ligand route. Start with one X outside, one Y inside and one empty wall site R. The events are X_out + R → A, Y_in + A → C, C → D, then D → B + X_in. The Y-binding step carries the X already on A. Every event balances X, Y and the scaffold. The engine’s host kernel gave rates of 0.00166054, 0.0166054, 1 and 1 per second at the four successive states. Following the enabled events left zero X outside and one X inside. Removing Y binding gave a sealed control that the parser admitted. This is a finite path with positive rates. The host check follows those events directly. [Tested parser](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/61d823be7e3913f44a4dd497507a8e8256151584/src/cells/models/assembly_lineages_v2/spec.py#L451-L505) · [Comparison driver](checks/shell_ligand_faces.py)

To repeat, set `PYTHONPATH` to the engine checkout’s `src` and root directories, in that order, and run `python -B lean/checks/shell_ligand_faces.py` with the Cells CPU environment. Its exit code is one when a row disagrees. The frozen engine, per-case JSON and logs are in the Lean run directory `lean-fr48-fr46-shell-laws-20261123`.

The earlier failed result remains recorded above. The new run directory `lean-fr47-wash-recovery-20261123` contains both machine-readable replays. ## 23 November 2026 · Tracer equilibrium in a prescribed shell

For `cells.shell_permeation@1` on `cells.prescribed_shell@1`, Lean checked the normalized count distribution for every fixed total, detailed balance across each entry/exit pair, and zero net probability flow for every count observable in continuous time. At equal volumes a lumen cost of ln 2 in units of RT gives a one-third fraction inside and a one-half ratio of mean concentrations. [Claim, assumptions and engine status](claims/fr48-shell-equilibrium.md)

With two tracers the exact probabilities at zero, one and two inside are 4/9, 4/9 and 1/9. With no tracer the only state has mass one. Setting the solution potential to ln 2 and the absolute lumen potential to ln 4 distinguishes the correct fraction (1/3) from using the lumen value as an offset (1/5) or reversing the gap (2/3). After the build, run `lake env lean -DwarningAsError=true checks/chemistry/ShellEquilibrium.lean` from `lean/` to repeat the controls and print the proof axioms. [Exact checks](checks/chemistry/ShellEquilibrium.lean)

On the frozen FR-48/FR-46 development stack at `61d823be`, the CPU test passed for 12 tracers. The stationary probabilities from the kernel rates agreed with the binomial reference within 10⁻¹² for a lumen cost of ln 2, equal potentials and the undeclared default. The test used a nonzero solution potential, detected an intentionally incorrect offset and closed the ledger from initially all-outside and all-inside populations. To repeat in that checkout, run `python -B -m pytest -q tests/unit/test_assembly_lineages_v2_geometry.py::test_a_lumen_potential_sets_the_equilibrium_ratio_and_keeps_detailed_balance`. [Engine comparison](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/61d823be7e3913f44a4dd497507a8e8256151584/tests/unit/test_assembly_lineages_v2_geometry.py#L806-L866)

## 23 November 2026 · Equilibrium around a closed reaction cycle

For the passive routes of `local_species_reactions@1`, Lean checked the sign of the equilibrium activity quotient, the zero standard-energy sum for real extents, the unit product for signed integer traversals, and the residual from changing one route’s energy. All four proofs use only Mathlib’s usual axioms (`propext`, `Classical.choice`, `Quot.sound`). The claim records the engine formula and the separate work needed to compare a compiled loop or measured equilibrium constants. [Proof and source register](claims/passive-reaction-cycles.md)

As a sign check, take the columns A → B, B → C and A → C in that order. Lean checked that traversals (2, 2, −2) return every species to its start and have product one for arbitrary species potentials. Adding an energy offset δ to the third route gives a residual of −2δ. Traversals (2, 2, 2) leave the cycle open. Run `lake env lean -DwarningAsError=true checks/chemistry/PassiveCycle.lean` from `lean/` after the build. [Exact example and axiom check](checks/chemistry/PassiveCycle.lean)

<a id="fr43-integer-segregation"></a>

## 23 November 2026 · Integer counts at membrane-patch division

For `cells.integer_unit_population@1`, Lean partitions each state's occupied indices using the strict draw threshold. The mask over a wide enough draw block gives the same set. The complementary count equals the engine's subtraction, including empty states and tied draws. Each state's two credits balance the retired parent's debit. Multiplying by the declared subunit weights and summing preserves every parent's total. This result holds for any realized draws. Two further results use independent trials to calculate the expected absolute difference between sister counts for two and four units. [Proofs](CellsProofs/Tissue/IntegerSegregation.lean) · [Operator contract](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/2e7bb39ba16b5a298193f8de71038c2ccc5318af/docs/library/cells/operators/integer_unit_population@1.md#L103)

On development engine `9663f28f`, the native composite ran the integer population beside membrane entity state and isopotential partition. Four sites on one patch started with 7, 6, 4 and 0 subunits. The first daughter's volume share was 0.5. Its contact-patch area share was 0.7525343617082912, exactly equal in binary64 to an occupied unit's partition draw. An independent Threefry reader and per-unit counts reproduced every parent, daughter and signed division entry at all six saved times. The 0.02 s row precedes division and the 0.03 s row contains the daughters. Their weighted counts were (4, 3), (6, 0), (3, 1) and (0, 0). Using volume share, sending a tie to the first daughter, or reversing state order changed 3, 1 and 2 first-daughter entries respectively. All three alternative allocations still conserved each state's total. Six targeted tests passed in the engine's 64-bit CPU mode. The proof and native records are in run `lean-fr43-integer-division-20261123`. [Native comparison](checks/integer_segregation.py) · [Independent draw reader](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/9663f28f36bc9d1595a5bafd08495a805b8dc650/validation/fr43_part3/references/stream_draws.py#L94)

The Cells biological evidence Search thread identified Coelho et al.'s fission-yeast images. In Movie S3, the upper-left parent has one visible Hsp104 focus at 120 min. At 130 min the daughters have one and zero. In Figure 2E, the two-focus group has mean absolute sister-count difference about 0.97 (33 divisions), close to 1 for independent equal allocation. The four-focus group gives about 1.0 (32 divisions). The SEM is about 0.18 in each group, read from the plot. The independent four-unit expectation is at least 1.5 for **any** common daughter probability, including unequal area shares. The observed four-focus mean is lower. These cytoplasmic aggregates fuse and their movement is spatially constrained. The experiment lacks matched membrane-area fractions. It gives a count comparison and a limit on applying the independent rule to aggregates. <a id="fr29-low-ph-current"></a>

### Low-pH reversal of the yeast vacuolar proton pump

At cytosolic pH 7.5 and lumen pH 3.1 the journal trace shows an inward ATP-dependent difference current over −80 to +40 mV. Table 2 gives a reversal above +40 mV (three vacuoles). An earlier workshop report by the same group lists +50 ± 8 mV (SE) without a replicate count. Its low-pH figure is a time trace at 0 mV, and its voltage ramp lasted 2.5 s compared with 5 s in the journal report. The search located neither a corresponding low-pH voltage sweep resolving that crossing nor an independent matched recording. The journal bound remains the comparator for FR-29. [Kettner et al., Fig. 2B and Table 2](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC1303676/) · [Workshop, Fig. 7B and Table 1](https://www.beilstein-institut.de/download/736/kettner_1.pdf#page=14) · [Measurement record](checks/data/kettner_low_ph_reversal.json) · [Project decision](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/61b418fc5f116c1e92d1ac50d5ae7b551cee05c9/docs/DECISIONS.md#L8598)

<a id="rosenbrock-reaction-inventory"></a>

## 23 November 2026 · Material conservation through an accepted reaction step

For `cells.rate_equations@1`, a linear inventory that balances every reaction also gives zero when applied to the Jacobian of the reaction field in any direction. Projecting each Rosenbrock stage solve onto it leaves zero change. The endpoint and the dense polynomial used to locate crossings therefore preserve the initial amount in exact arithmetic. This argument assumes differentiability, balanced reactions throughout the step, a nonzero shift and exact stage solves. Material crossing the modeled boundary or changing at an event needs its own accounting. The proof imports Mathlib’s chain rule and uniqueness of derivatives. [Five Lean declarations](CellsProofs/Chemistry/RosenbrockInventory.lean) · [Compiled stage and dense formulas](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/4944314d4d8b77c6a42ce1441503ac3e51de9dcb/src/cells/numerics/rosenbrock_dense.py#L123)

In a native CPU float64 run of A + B ⇌ C, the initial inventories were 11,000 A units and 3,000 B units. The first accepted step matched the first saved observation bit for bit. Its six stages had a maximum inventory residual of 6.25 × 10⁻¹⁷ molecule units and its dense polynomial drifted by at most 1.82 × 10⁻¹² at five sampled fractions. Across four saved observation times and 64 accepted steps, the largest relative drift was 9.10 × 10⁻¹⁶, below the native 10⁻¹² tolerance. Counting each molecule once instead changed the total by 653.29. The run `lean-rosenbrock-inventory-20261123` stores `rosenbrock-inventory.json`, both native bundles, the frozen source and the targeted tests. [Reproducible driver](checks/rosenbrock_inventory.py) · [Engine material accounts](https://github.com/Eidosoma/Cells/blob/4944314d4d8b77c6a42ce1441503ac3e51de9dcb/src/cells/models/assembly_lineages_v2/rate_equations.py#L1866)

In a partially purified rabbit-muscle phosphoglucose isomerase assay, reported G6P + F6P was 2.500, 2.500 and 2.505 mM at 0, 15 and 30 minutes. The native closed-pair run started from the first measurement with both sugars present, at 15 minutes, and predicted 2.500 mM at 30 minutes. The measured excess was 0.005 mM (0.2%). The paper gives no replicate uncertainty for the sum. In two *E. coli* extract time courses the same two measured sugars went from 6.8872 to 5.3523 mM (−22.29%) and from 9.5845 to 9.6838 mM (+1.04%). The workbook gives no pointwise uncertainty or covariance. The first extract course limits treating these two measured species as a closed pool. Both experiments inform the physical assumption. Neither measures solver error. [Kahana et al., Table II, experiment B](https://doi.org/10.1016/S0021-9258(18)64594-6) · [Eicher et al., Fig. 5](https://doi.org/10.3390/metabo2040818) · [PGI worksheet](https://mdpi-res.com/metabolites/metabolites-02-00818/article_deploy/metabolites-02-00818-s001.zip)
